<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增强-检测方法无法满足自动驾驶的安全实时性要求，且存在标注完整性偏差：在退化图像上标注的GT无法奖励那些恢复出标注者不可见目标的检测器，导致真正有用的增强在F1指标上近乎无提升。

Method: 采用三线程架构：Thread S（YOLOv11n）以全帧率零延迟检测；Thread Q进行条件自适应增强（CAPE）并通过熵引导NMS（EG-NMS）融合结果而不阻塞S线程；Thread E使用CLIP零样本天气分类，新天气类别只需新文本提示，无需标注数据和重新训练。

Result: 在1327张DAWN图像上（YOLOv11m, IoU=0.5, 置信度=0.25），Recall=0.0103（micro），雪天F1=0.0230，雨天F1=0.0038。Thread S保持约44 FPS，不受增强负载影响。

Conclusion: CADENet无需模型重新训练或额外传感器硬件，即可在恶劣天气下实现实时目标检测，并指出F1值仅是真实增益的下界，Recall是免受标注缺口影响的头号指标。

Abstract: Adverse weather (rain, fog, sand, and snow) degrades camera-based object detection in autonomous vehicles. Existing enhancement-then-detect approaches stall the safety-critical perception loop, violating hard real-time requirements. Progress on this problem is also constrained by an under-recognized evaluation ceiling: ground truth annotated on degraded images cannot credit a detector that recovers objects the annotators themselves could not see, so a genuinely useful enhancement can register as a near-flat F1 gain. This paper presents CADENet (Condition-Adaptive Asynchronous Dual-stream Enhancement Network), a training-free three-thread system: Thread S (YOLOv11n) delivers detections at full frame rate with zero added latency; Thread Q applies condition-adaptive enhancement (CAPE) and fuses results via entropy-guided NMS (EG-NMS) without blocking Thread S; Thread E provides CLIP zero-shot weather classification, so new weather categories require only a new text prompt, with no labeled data and no retraining. Evaluated on 1327 DAWN images (YOLOv11m, IoU = 0.5, confidence = 0.25), CADENet achieves Recall = 0.0103 (micro), F1 = 0.0230 on snow, and F1 = 0.0038 on rain. We formalize the annotation completeness bias on DAWN-class data, so the reported F1 values are lower bounds on the true gain; recall is the annotation-gap-immune headline metric. Thread S sustains approximately 44 FPS regardless of enhancement load. No model retraining or additional sensor hardware is required.

</details>


### [39] [deadtrees.earth-aerial: A Multi-Resolution Aerial Image Dataset for Tree Cover and Mortality Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19605)
*Ayushi Sharma,Clemens Mosig,Lukas Drees,Salim Soltani,Janusch Vajna-Jehle,Aaron Sheppard,Belqis Ahmadi,Jonathan Schmid,Paul Neumeier,Nathan Jacobs,Jan Dirk Wegner,Teja Kattenborn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了两个新的开放机器学习数据集DTE-aerial-train和DTE-aerial-bench，用于从厘米级航拍影像中联合分割树冠覆盖和树木死亡率，覆盖全球多种生物群落，并建立了强基准基线，在北方森林等挑战性区域实现了约45%的F1分数提升（从0.40到0.58）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球森林面临气候变化和火灾、病虫害等干扰的日益严重威胁，迫切需要可扩展的树木覆盖和死亡率监测方法。然而，缺乏全球代表性、统一的数据集来联合分割树冠和死亡率，限制了相关进展。

Method: 作者构建了两个新数据集：DTE-aerial-train（385K个1024x1024像素的图像块，分辨率2.5-20厘米，包含专家标注的树冠和死亡率伪标签）和DTE-aerial-bench（25个全球分布的25个正射影像，共525个图块，含高质量专家标注）。数据集覆盖热带、温带、北方和干旱生物群落。基于基准测试集建立了参考基线。

Result: 在基准测试集上建立的基线在 mortality 分割上表现优异，在所有生物群落和尺度上均有提升，特别是在北方森林等挑战性区域，F1分数从0.40提升至0.58，相对提升约45%。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了全球尺度下从厘米级航拍影像联合分割树冠覆盖和树木死亡率，并公开发布所有数据、模型和代码，为森林监测研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: Forests worldwide are increasingly threatened by climate change and disturbances such as fire, pests, and pathogens, creating an urgent need for scalable monitoring of tree cover and tree mortality. Aerial imagery from drones and aircraft is a key data source for detailed and large-scale mapping of tree crowns and mortality. However, related progress is limited by the lack of globally representative, harmonized datasets for joint segmentation of tree cover and mortality. We introduce two novel, open, machine-learning-ready datasets to enable joint segmentation of tree cover and tree mortality from centimeter-scale aerial imagery for the first time at global scales. With DTE-aerial-train, we provide a training dataset comprising 385K image patches of size 1024x1024 pixels, with resolutions ranging from 2.5 to 20 cm. It includes multi-class expert-annotated and -audited pseudo-labels for tree cover and mortality. With DTE-aerial-bench, we provide a geographically balanced benchmark test set of 25 globally distributed orthoimages totaling 525 patches with high-quality expert annotations for both tree cover and mortality. Both the training and benchmark datasets span tropical, temperate, boreal, and dryland biomes and cover a wide range of forest structures and mortality patterns. Using the benchmark test set for evaluation, we establish strong reference baselines that improve mortality segmentation across all biomes and scales with significant gains in challenging regions, such as boreal forests, where the F1 score increases from 0.40 to 0.58 with around 45% relative improvement. All data, models, and code will be publicly released under permissive open-source licenses. An interactive visualization of the benchmark dataset is available at deadtrees.earth/releases/dte-aerial-bench.

</details>


### [40] [FineBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Vision-Language Models for Fine-grained Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19846)
*Gueter Josmy Faure,Min-Hung Chen,Jia-Fong Yeh,Hung-Ting Su,Winston H. Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FineBench，一个大规模、细粒度的人类中心视频问答基准（199,420个QA对，覆盖64部长视频），以及FineAgent模块化框架，通过定位器和描述器提升开源视觉语言模型在细粒度视频理解上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在通用视频理解上表现良好，但在细粒度人类行为理解（如动作细节、多人交互、物体操作）方面存在不足；当前基准缺乏结合长视频、密集QA覆盖和帧级时空定位的大规模评估。

Method: 构建FineBench基准（199,420个多选题，64部15分钟长视频，聚焦人物运动、交互、物体操作），并提出FineAgent模块化框架，包含Localizer（定位关键时空区域）和Descriptor（生成细粒度描述）来增强开源VLM。

Result: 闭源模型（如GPT-5）表现尚可，但开源VLM显著较差，尤其在多人场景空间推理和细微动作区分上；FineAgent框架能持续提升多种开源VLM在FineBench上的性能。

Conclusion: FineBench为细粒度人类中心视频理解提供了严格的测试平台，FineAgent为当前VLM提供了实用的增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in general video understanding, yet they often struggle with the fine-grained comprehension crucial for real-world applications requiring nuanced interpretation of human actions and interactions. While some recent human-centric benchmarks evaluate aspects of model behaviour such as fairness/ethics, emotion perception, and broader human-centric metrics, they do not combine long-form videos, very dense QA coverage, and frame-level spatial/temporal grounding at scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce FineBench, a human-centric video question answering (VQA) benchmark specifically designed to assess fine-grained understanding. FineBench comprises 199,420 multiple-choice QA pairs densely annotated across 64 long-form videos (15 minutes each), focusing on detailed person movement, person interaction, and object manipulation, including compositional actions. Our extensive evaluation reveals that while proprietary models like GPT-5 achieve respectable performance, current open-source VLMs significantly underperform, struggling particularly with spatial reasoning in multi-person scenes and distinguishing subtle differences in human movements and interactions. To address these identified weaknesses, we propose FineAgent, a modular framework that enhances VLMs by leveraging a Localizer and a Descriptor. Experiments show that FineAgent consistently improves the performance of various open VLMs on FineBench. FineBench provides a rigorous testbed for future research into fine-grained human-centric video understanding, while FineAgent offers a practical approach to enhance such reasoning in current VLMs.

</details>


### [41] [Spectral Integrated Gradients for Coarse-to-Fine Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19607)
*Soyeon Kim,Seongwoo Lim,Kyowoon Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Integrated Gradients (SIG)，通过基于SVD的积分路径替代传统直线路径，按奇异值从大到小逐步激活特征，实现从粗到细的特征归因，获得更干净、噪声更少的归因图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Integrated Gradients使用直线积分路径，所有输入特征同时引入，导致梯度噪声累积，影响归因质量。

Method: 基于基线到输入差异的奇异值分解(SVD)构建积分路径，按奇异值从大到小逐步激活成分，形成从全局结构到细节的渐进式归因。

Result: 在多个图像分类数据集上，SIG生成更干净的归因图，噪声更少，定量指标优于现有基于路径的归因方法。

Conclusion: SVD引导的积分路径能有效减少噪声累积，提升归因质量，验证了从粗到细渐进式归因的有效性。

Abstract: Integrated Gradients (IG) is a widely adopted feature attribution method that satisfies desirable axiomatic properties. However, the choice of integration path significantly affects the quality of attributions, and the standard straight-line path introduces all input features simultaneously, often accumulating noisy gradients along the way. To address this limitation, we propose Spectral Integrated Gradients, which constructs integration paths based on singular value decomposition (SVD) of the baseline-to-input difference. By progressively activating singular components from largest to smallest, SIG introduces global structure before fine-grained details, naturally following a coarse-to-fine progression. Through extensive evaluation across diverse image classification datasets, we demonstrate that SIG produces cleaner attribution maps with reduced noise and achieves improved quantitative performance compared to existing path-based attribution methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/leekwoon/sig/.

</details>


### [42] [Rethinking Visual Attribution for Chest X-ray Reasoning in Large Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20158)
*Guangzhi Xiong,Qiao Jin,Sanchit Sinha,Zhiyong Lu,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过因果评估框架发现现有视觉归因方法无法可靠反映LVLM在胸部X光片推理中的真实证据，并提出MedFocus——一种基于概念（非均衡最优传输）的归因方法，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM在医学应用中无法忠实基于视觉证据进行推理，现有归因方法是否反映模型真实决策证据未经验证，缺乏因果层面的评估框架。

Method: 构建因果评估框架，通过反事实编辑验证专家标注区域是否因果性地影响模型预测；提出MedFocus，利用非均衡最优传输定位临床解剖区域，并通过目标干预测量其对模型输出的因果效应，生成空间、概念和token三级归因。

Result: 在11种归因方法、6个开源LVLM和两种输出模式上测试，发现现有方法常无法识别LVLM使用的证据；MedFocus在各项指标上大幅优于已有方法。

Conclusion: 现有视觉归因方法对医学LVLM不可靠，MedFocus通过概念级因果归因显著提升可信度，朝着更可信的医学LVLM解释迈进一步。

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) show promise in medical applications, but their inability to faithfully ground responses in visual evidence raises serious concerns about clinical trustworthiness. While visual attribution methods are widely used to explain LVLM predictions, whether these explanations actually reflect the visual evidence underlying the model's decision is largely unverified, since ground-truth annotations for internal model reasoning are typically unavailable. We address this question for chest X-ray (CXR) reasoning by developing a causal evaluation framework that retains only CXR-VQA samples for which the expert-annotated region is verified, via counterfactual editing, to be causally responsible for the model's prediction. Using this framework across 11 attribution methods, six open-source LVLMs, and two output modes (direct answer and step-by-step reasoning), we find that existing attribution methods often fail to identify the evidence used by LVLMs. To address this failure, we propose MedFocus, a concept-based attribution method that localizes clinically meaningful anatomical regions via unbalanced optimal transport and measures their causal effect on model outputs through targeted interventions. MedFocus produces spatial, concept-level, and token-level attributions and substantially outperforms prior methods, taking a step toward more trustworthy attribution for medical LVLMs. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/gzxiong/medfocus/.

</details>


### [43] [White-Balance First, Adjust Later: Cross-Camera Color Constancy via Vision-Language Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19613)
*Shuwei Li,Lei Tan,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VLM-CC框架，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）的感知反馈，将颜色恒常性建模为迭代校正过程，在跨相机场景下实现鲁棒的白平衡估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的颜色恒常性方法在跨相机场景下泛化能力差，容易过拟合到训练相机的色彩响应特性，导致在其他相机拍摄的图像上性能下降。

Method: VLM-CC将颜色恒常性转化为迭代精化过程：每一步先基于当前照明估计进行白平衡校正并转换为伪sRGB，然后利用轻量级LoRA微调的VLM评估校正后图像，识别残留色偏并提供定性反馈，再将反馈映射为残差照明方向（R/G/B），更新照明估计，迭代至收敛。

Result: 在多个数据集上，VLM-CC实现了跨相机颜色恒常性中目前最优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将颜色恒常性重构为迭代感知反馈问题，用VLM评估替代直接RGB回归，有效提升了跨相机泛化能力。

Abstract: Color constancy aims to keep object colors consistent under varying illumination. Cross-camera generalization in color constancy remains challenging because learning-based models often overfit to the color response characteristics of the training camera, resulting in degraded performance on images captured by other cameras. We propose VLM-CC, a feedback-guided framework that formulates color constancy as an iterative refinement process. Instead of directly estimating the illuminant from raw input, VLM-CC performs iterative correction driven by vision-language model (VLM)-based evaluation. At each iteration, the image is white-balanced using the current estimate and converted to pseudo-sRGB. A lightweight LoRA-tuned VLM then assesses the corrected image, identifying the dominant residual color cast and providing qualitative feedback. This feedback is mapped to a residual illumination direction (red, green, or blue) and used to update the illuminant estimate until convergence. Our key idea is to reframe color constancy as an iterative perceptual feedback problem, leveraging VLM evaluation instead of direct RGB regression. By replacing direct RGB estimation with VLM-guided perceptual feedback, VLM-CC achieves state-of-the-art robustness in cross-camera color constancy across multiple datasets. Code will be available at https://github.com/NothingIknow/VLM-CC.

</details>


### [44] [Bézier Degradation Modeling for LiDAR-based Human Motion Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19620)
*Xiaoqi An,Lin Zhao,Jun Li,Chen Gong,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BMLiCap提出了一种基于贝塞尔曲线的粗到细框架，通过时间可压缩的贝塞尔曲线建模运动，结合多尺度时间运动变换器（TMT）和多级运动聚合器（MMA），在多个LiDAR人体运动基准上实现了最先进的精度和时间连续性，有效解决了遮挡和噪声导致的抖动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的3D人体运动捕捉方法在处理不稳定输入和严重遮挡时，容易出现预测抖动甚至失败的问题。

Method: 提出BMLiCap粗到细框架，使用时间可压缩贝塞尔曲线建模人体运动，通过轨迹保持策略减少控制点；设计渐进式运动重建模块，包括多尺度时间运动变换器（TMT）预测多时间尺度运动曲线，以及多级运动聚合器（MMA）自适应融合多尺度曲线。

Result: 在LiDARHuman26M、FreeMotion、NoiseMotion和SLOPER4D四个基准上取得了最先进的精度和时间连续性，能够补偿严重遮挡并减少预测抖动。

Conclusion: BMLiCap通过贝塞尔曲线表示和多尺度融合策略，有效解决了LiDAR人体运动捕捉中的遮挡和噪声问题，为复杂场景下的3D人体运动重建提供了一种鲁棒且高精度的解决方案。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D human motion capture has broad applications in fields such as autonomous driving and robotics, where accurate motion reconstruction is crucial. However, existing methods often struggle with unstable inputs and severe occlusions, leading to jittery or even failed pose predictions. To address these challenges, we propose BMLiCap, a coarse-to-fine framework that models motion using temporally compressible Bézier curves. By reducing control points through a trajectory-preserving strategy, we obtain a coherent and learning-friendly motion representation. To reconstruct human actions from LiDAR point-cloud cues, we design a progressive motion-reconstruction module. Specifically, a Time-scale Motion Transformer (TMT) is introduced to predict motion curves at multiple temporal scales, and a Multi-level Motion Aggregator (MMA) is utilized to adaptively fuse the multi-scale curves to recover detailed, temporally coherent poses, effectively bridging observation gaps caused by occlusions and noise. Across four mainstream benchmarks LiDARHuman26M, FreeMotion, NoiseMotion, and SLOPER4D, BMLiCap achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and temporal continuity in complex scenes, demonstrating its ability to compensate for severe occlusions and reduce prediction jitter.

</details>


### [45] [UniRefiner: Teaching Pre-trained ViTs to Self-Dispose Dross via Contrastive Register](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19622)
*Congpei Qiu,Zhaoyu Hu,Wei Ke,Zhuotao Tian,Yanhao Wu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UniRefiner，一种通用精炼框架，通过对比注册机制让预训练 ViT 模型自我识别并清除三类空间伪影 token，仅需微调约 5k 图像即可显著提升密集预测任务性能，如 EVA-CLIP-8B 在 ADE20K 上达到 51.9% mIoU（+9.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 ViT 模型在大规模密集预测任务中受到伪影 token（spurious tokens）的严重干扰，而先前的研究对这类伪影的定义过于狭隘（仅视为高范数异常点），未能覆盖更广泛的破坏空间对齐语义的 token 类型，限制了预训练模型在空间敏感任务中的潜力。

Method: 提出 UniRefiner 框架，包含三个步骤：首先系统分类定义三类空间伪影 token；然后引入对比注册（contrastive registers）机制，通过双目标优化——对齐图像 token 与过滤后的正则 token 以保持语义，对齐注册 token 与检测到的伪影 token 以捕获伪影信号——实现对伪影 token 的隔离与重分布；仅需在约 5k 图像上微调数个 epoch。

Result: 在 ADE20K 上 EVA-CLIP-8B 达到 51.9% mIoU（+9.4%），超越 DINOv2（49.1%）；零样本分割准确率提升高达 22%；适用于包括 EVA-CLIP-8B 和 InternViT-6B 等多种大规模 ViT 模型。

Conclusion: UniRefiner 有效解锁了现有大规模基础模型在空间感知任务中的潜在能力，为将通用预训练模型推广到更广泛的空间密集预测应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Representation learning with Vision Transformers (ViTs) has advanced rapidly, yet the utility of large-scale models in spatially sensitive tasks is hindered by spurious tokens. Prior efforts to mitigate this have been limited, often defining these artifacts narrowly, for example, as simple high-norm outliers. We argue that this scope is insufficient. For dense prediction tasks, we posit that any token failing to encode location-aligned semantics should be treated as a spurious artifact. This broader definition reveals a more complex problem, leading us to systematically categorize and characterize three fundamental types of spurious tokens that corrupt spatial representations. Based on this comprehensive diagnosis, we propose UniRefiner, a universal refinement framework that teaches pre-trained ViTs to self-dispose of these artifacts. UniRefiner uses contrastive registers to explicitly isolate and redistribute spurious tokens via a dual objective: (i) it aligns image tokens with filtered regular tokens to preserve semantics, and (ii) it aligns register tokens with detected spurious tokens to capture the spurious signals. Our method requires only a few epochs of fine-tuning on ~5k images to refine diverse ViTs, including massive models like EVA-CLIP-8B and InternViT-6B. Experiments demonstrate consistent and significant improvements: notably, the refined EVA-CLIP-8B achieves 51.9\% mIoU on ADE20K (+9.4\%), surpassing specialized vision models like DINOv2 (49.1\%), while zero-shot segmentation accuracy improves by up to 22\%. UniRefiner unlocks the latent spatial potential of existing large-scale foundation models, paving the way for their broader application.

</details>


### [46] [PrAda: Few-Shot Visual Adaptation for Text-Prompted Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19623)
*Gabriele Rosi,Fabio Cermelli,Carlo Masone,Barbara Caputo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PrAda方法，通过原型适应解决文本提示分割模型在域迁移时的分类错误问题，在少样本场景下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本提示分割模型在目标域（与预训练数据差异大的领域）上性能下降，研究发现主要原因是分类错误而非掩码生成错误。同时，少样本视觉适应在图像分类中研究广泛，但在分割任务中尚未探索。

Method: 提出Prototype Adaptation (PrAda)，一种参数高效的方法：学习类别特定原型，结合细粒度像素特征和高级Transformer表示，通过学习的重要性因子与原始文本预测融合，保持零样本潜力同时适应新领域。

Result: 在语义、实例和全景分割五个基准上，PrAda显著优于现有最先进方法和所提出的基线。

Conclusion: PrAda有效解决了文本提示分割模型在域迁移下的分类错误问题，为少样本分割适应提供了新范式。

Abstract: Segmenting images is critical for visual understanding but demands extensive pixel-level annotations. Foundational models have enabled new paradigms for predicting new classes guided by textual prompts, without annotations from the target domain. Yet, on specialized target domains, far from the original pre-training, their performance degrades. We study the errors of existing methods under such domain-shift, finding that misclassification rather than mask generation is the main culprit. To address this, we introduce the novel problem of Few-Shot Visual Adaptation for text-prompted Segmentation. This kind of adaptation has been largely studied for image classification, but it remains unexplored for segmentation. We tackle this task with Prototype Adaptation (PrAda), a novel, parameter-efficient method that adapts a frozen text-prompted segmentation model. Our approach learns class-specific prototypes by combining fine-grained pixel features and high-level transformer representations, which are then fused with the original text-based predictions through a learned importance factor. This preserves the model's zero-shot potential while enabling strong adaptation to new domains. Experiments across semantic, instance, and panoptic segmentation on five benchmarks demonstrate that PrAda yields significant improvements over state-of-the-art and proposed baselines.

</details>


### [47] [Component-Aware Structure-Preserving Style Transfer for Satellite Sim2Real 6D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19624)
*Yonglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向卫星单目6D姿态估计的部件级结构保持风格迁移框架，通过将合成图像的几何标注与真实域外观结合，在Sim2Real场景下有效提升了姿态估计精度，FID达54.32，ADD通过率提升至0.260。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作卫星的单目6D姿态估计高度依赖带标注训练数据，但真实卫星图像难以大规模获取精确的位姿标签和部件级掩码，而纯合成数据与真实图像之间存在明显的域间外观差异，限制了模型在真实场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 构建弱配对真实-合成样本（通过标定采集、ArUco相机位姿测量、CAD渲染和部件掩码）；从无标签真实图像提取部件级风格编码，通过掩码对齐的调制注入到合成图像的对应卫星部件区域；结合对抗训练、局部对比一致性约束、自正则化和边缘保持约束来确保生成图像的可用性。

Result: 在5000张渲染图像和100张真实图像上实验，相比代表性图像翻译基线，方法取得最低域差异（FID 54.32, KID 0.048）；下游GDRNet姿态估计器在翻译数据上训练后，ADD通过率达0.260，AUC达0.611。

Conclusion: 部件级外观迁移能够在保留合成数据几何标注的同时，有效提升卫星Sim2Real姿态估计性能。

Abstract: Monocular 6D pose estimation for non-cooperative satellites depends heavily on annotated training data, yet real satellite images with reliable pose labels and component-level masks are difficult to acquire at scale. Synthetic rendering can provide exact geometric annotations, but the appearance gap between rendered and real observations limits direct transfer to the real domain. This paper presents a component-aware structure-preserving style transfer framework for satellite synthetic-to-real data construction. The method builds weakly paired real--synthetic samples from calibrated real acquisition, ArUco-based camera-pose measurement, CAD rendering, and component masks. It then extracts part-wise real-domain style codes from unlabeled real images and injects them into corresponding synthetic satellite regions through mask-aligned modulation. To keep the generated images usable for downstream supervision, adversarial training is combined with local contrastive consistency, self-regularization, and edge-preserving constraints. Experiments are conducted on 5,000 rendered satellite images and 100 real images captured in a calibrated setup. The real images provide target-domain appearance references and final evaluation images, while the downstream GDRNet pose estimator is trained only on synthetic or translated synthetic images. Compared with representative image-translation baselines, the proposed method achieves the lowest image distribution discrepancy, with an FID of 54.32 and a KID of 0.048. When the translated data are used to train GDRNet in this target-domain adaptation setting, the ADD pass rate improves to 0.260 and the AUC improves to 0.611. These results indicate that component-level appearance transfer can improve satellite Sim2Real pose estimation in the considered calibrated setup while retaining simulation-derived geometric annotations.

</details>


### [48] [P2DNav: Panorama-to-Downview Reasoning for Zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19634)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Haojie Dai,Jinlong Li,Yongrui Qin,Zongtao He,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P2DNav层次化框架，通过全景到俯视图的显式分解、滑动窗口对话记忆和反射性重定向机制，在零样本视觉语言导航任务中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本VLN方法依赖额外的航点预测模块，往往将高层方向推理与细粒度局部定位纠缠在一起，导致决策易出错且不稳定。

Method: 提出P2DNav框架，包含三个核心组件：P2D（将导航决策分解为全景方向选择和俯视图局部定位两阶段）、SDM（用滑动窗口对话记忆组织历史导航信息）、RRM（通过评估局部定位可靠性实现反射性重定向）。

Result: 在R2R-CE基准上，与SOTA零样本基于航点的方法相比成功率提升146.6%，与无航点方法相比提升58.9%。

Conclusion: P2D、SDM和RRM三个组件在零样本VLN中均有效，该框架性能显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to ground natural-language instructions into executable navigation actions in unseen environments. Existing zero-shot methods typically rely on additional waypoint prediction modules, which often entangle high-level directional reasoning with fine-grained local grounding, leading to error-prone and unstable decisions. In this paper, we propose P2DNav, a hierarchical framework for zero-shot vision-and-language navigation. P2DNav consists of three core components: Panorama-to-Downview (P2D), Sliding-Window Dialogue Memory (SDM), and Reflective Reorientation Mechanism (RRM). P2D explicitly decomposes navigation decision-making into two stages: panoramic direction selection and downview local grounding. It first selects the instruction-relevant direction from a 360° panorama, and then predicts a pixel-level target point from the downview RGB observation in that direction. In addition, SDM organizes navigation history as a multi-turn dialogue context and maintains recent visual observations within a sliding window to support long-horizon navigation. RRM further enables reflective reorientation by assessing the reliability of local grounding based on the downview observation and returning to panoramic direction selection when necessary. Experiments on the R2R-CE benchmark show that P2DNav achieves strong performance among zero-shot methods. In particular, compared with the state-of-the-art (SOTA) zero-shot waypoint-based and waypoint-free methods, P2DNav achieves SR gains of 146.6% and 58.9%, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of P2D, SDM, and RRM for zero-shot VLN. Code will be released for public use.

</details>


### [49] [Benchmarking and Evolving Reason-Reflect-Rectify for Reflective Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19639)
*Junjie Wang,Xinghua Lou,Jason Li,Ye Tian,Keyu Chen,Yulin Li,Bin Kang,Jacky Mai,Yanwei Li,Zhuotao Tian,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R^3循环框架解决文本到图像模型在多轮反思性视觉生成中的不足，构建R^3-Bench基准，并提出R^3-Refiner双阶段方法，通过GRPO和分层奖励机制显著提升模型在复杂提示下的迭代修正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型和统一多模态模型依赖单次生成范式，无法处理需要迭代修正的复杂提示，缺乏多轮反思性视觉生成能力。

Method: 形式化定义Reason-Reflect-Rectify (R^3)循环框架；构建R^3-Bench基准（600+专家标注实例）；提出R^3-Refiner双阶段框架，采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)和Hierarchical Reward Mechanism (HRM)对齐修正与反思推理。

Result: 在R^3-Bench上反思判定分数提升12.0%，修正分数提升9.0%；在GenEval++和T2I-CompBench上提升不同T2I模型的生成质量。

Conclusion: R^3-Refiner能有效弥合模型识别错误与生成可操作修正指令之间的差距，且可无缝集成多种多模态大语言模型以提升生成质量。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models and Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual generation. However, their reliance on a single-pass generation paradigm limits their ability to handle complex prompts requiring iterative refinement. To enable multi-round Reflective Visual Generation (RVG), we formalize the Reason-Reflect-Rectify (R^3) loop as a core framework and introduce R^3-Bench, a benchmark of over 600 expert-annotated instances that quantifies iterative reasoning and rectification capabilities. Evaluation on R^3-Bench reveals a critical gap: while state-of-the-art models can identify generation errors, they fail to generate actionable rectification instructions. To bridge this gap, we propose R^3-Refiner, a dual-stage framework leveraging Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and a Hierarchical Reward Mechanism (HRM) to better align rectification with reflective reasoning. Experiments show that R^3-Refiner achieves significant improvements on R^3-Bench (+12.0% in Reflective Verdict Score, +9.0% in Rectification Score), and can be seamlessly integrated with various MLLMs to enhance the generation quality of different T2I models on GenEval++ and T2I-CompBench. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaomoguhz/R3-Bench.

</details>


### [50] [Cross-View Splatter: Feed-Forward View Synthesis with Georeferenced Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19656)
*Matias Turkulainen,Akshay Krishnan,Filippo Aleotti,Mohamed Sayed,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Juho Kannala,Arno Solin,Gabriel Brostow,Daniyar Turmukhambetov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cross-View Splatter提出一种前馈式方法，融合地面照片与卫星图像，预测像素对齐的高斯泼溅，用于大规模室外场景的新视角合成和3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面图像采集耗时且难以覆盖大规模室外场景，而卫星图像易于获取但缺乏细节。本文旨在结合两者的互补优势，利用卫星图像提供的全局几何先验来弥补地面图像的覆盖不足。

Method: 方法融合了正射校正后的卫星视图与GPS标记的地面照片，在统一3D坐标系下预测高斯泼溅。通过对齐地面和鸟瞰视角的特征表示，实现场景覆盖和视角合成的改进。

Result: 在新建的带地理参考图像的基准上，相比仅使用地面图像的方法，场景覆盖和新视角合成效果均有提升，并优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 跨视角融合卫星与地面图像能有效提升室外场景的重建质量，代码和数据将开源。

Abstract: We present Cross-View Splatter, a feed-forward method that predicts pixel-aligned Gaussian splats for outdoor scenes captured at ground level AND by satellite. Faithful reconstructions require good camera coverage, but ground imagery is time-consuming and hard to capture at scale for large outdoor scenes. Fortunately, satellite imagery can provide a global geometric prior that is easy to access via public APIs. Cross-View Splatter fuses orthorectified satellite views with GPS-tagged ground photos to predict Gaussian splats in a unified 3D coordinate frame. By aligning ground and bird's-eye feature representations, our model improves scene coverage and novel-view synthesis, compared to ground imagery alone. We train on curated georeferenced datasets and paired satellite-terrain data, mined from open mapping services. We evaluate our method on a new benchmark for novel-view synthesis with georeferenced imagery allowing comparison to prior state-of-the-art methods. Our code and data preparation will be available at https://nianticspatial.github.io/cross-view-splatter/.

</details>


### [51] [WBCAtt+: Fine-Grained Pixel-Level Morphological Annotations for White Blood Cell Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19692)
*Satoshi Tsutsui,Winnie Pang,Shuting He,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了WBCAtt+数据集，首次为白细胞图像提供了11种形态属性和5种像素级细胞组分的密集标注，并基于此建立了属性识别和语义分割的基线模型，支持可解释AI等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白细胞图像数据集仅标注细胞类别，缺乏病理学家用于解释细胞解读的详细形态特征，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建WBCAtt+数据集，包含113k图像级标签和10k分割图，提供11种形态属性和5种像素级细胞组分标注；设计考虑细胞组合结构的属性识别模型，并建立基线模型。

Result: WBCAtt+是首个提供白细胞图像综合标注的数据集；所提出的属性识别模型在性能上有所提升；支持可解释AI、反事实样本生成等多种应用。

Conclusion: WBCAtt+数据集及其基线模型为白细胞图像分析提供了更丰富的标注资源，有助于推动可解释的血液疾病诊断研究。

Abstract: The microscopic examination of white blood cells (WBCs) plays a fundamental role in pathology and is essential for diagnosing blood disorders such as leukemia and anemia. To support further research on WBC images, multiple datasets have been proposed. However, they mainly annotate cell categories, and lack detailed morphological characteristics that pathologists use to explain their interpretations of cells. To address this gap, we introduce WBCAtt+, a novel dataset of WBC images densely annotated with 11 morphological attributes and five pixel-level cell components. With 113k image-level labels and 10k segmentation maps, WBCAtt+ is the first to provide comprehensive annotations for WBC images. Leveraging this dataset, we provide baseline models for attribute recognition and semantic segmentation. We also design an attribute recognition model to incorporate compositional structure of cells, further improving the recognition performance. Lastly, we showcase various applications enabled by our dataset, such as explainable AI models, including counterfactual example generation. \revision{The dataset and code are publicly available\footnote{https://doi.org/10.57967/hf/8143}}.

</details>


### [52] [Efficient Long-Context Modeling in Diffusion Language Models via Block Approximate Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19726)
*Wenhu Zhang,Yiming Wu,Huanyu Wang,Yaoyang Liu,Huanzhang Dou,Senqiao Yang,Sitong Wu,Hanbin Zhao,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BA-Att框架，通过块级预下采样操作和轻量级范数排序模块，在保持近似全注意力的性能下，实现最高6.95倍的注意力计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在超长序列下计算成本高昂，现有块稀疏注意力方法依赖固定采样模式（如尾部区域或反对角条纹），容易遗漏关键token且在分布偏移下不稳定。

Method: 提出BA-Att框架，包含块级预下采样操作、轻量级范数排序模块，以及利用对角QK方差近似全协方差的协方差补偿校正方法。

Result: 在注意力计算上比FlashAttention加速最高6.95倍；在50%稀疏度下，在语言模型、多模态语言模型和视频生成模型上均保持接近全注意力的性能。

Conclusion: BA-Att通过避免依赖脆弱的先验位置模式，在高效性和泛化性上均表现优异，为扩散语言模型的长序列生成提供了一种有效的稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) enable globally coherent, bidirectional, and controllable text generation, offering advantages over traditional autoregressive LLMs, while scaling to ultra-long sequences remains costly. Many existing block-sparse attention methods select blocks by fixed sampling patterns over the high-resolution attention space, such as tail regions or anti-diagonal stripes. Such prior-driven sampling can miss salient tokens and introduce instability under distribution shifts. In this paper, we propose the Block Approximate Sparse Attention framework (BA-Att) with block-wise pre-downsampled operation, which identifies informative regions within a compact downsampled space, avoiding reliance on brittle positional priors. To analyze its theoretical behavior, we define an oracle post-downsample attention map and formalize the approximation error between pre- and post-downsample schemes. Based on this insight, we introduce a lightweight norm-sorting module and a covariance-compensated correction that approximates full covariance using diagonal QK variances, reducing computational complexity. Extensive experiments show that our operator achieves up to 6.95x acceleration over FlashAttention in attention computation, and maintains near full-attention performance at 50% sparsity across language models, multimodal language models, and video generation models, demonstrating strong efficiency and generalization.

</details>


### [53] [Tango3D: Towards Alignment for Global and Local 2D-3D Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19727)
*Zebin He,Mingxin Yang,Shuhui Yang,Hanxiao Sun,Xintong Han,Chunchao Guo,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tango3D是一个统一密集对应与全局检索的3D基础模型，通过几何感知的2D视觉骨干和3D VAE实现像素到点的细粒度对齐，同时保持全局语义对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型仅将点云与视觉-语言空间（如CLIP）进行全局对齐，无法建立细粒度的像素到点对应关系，限制了密集3D下游任务的应用。

Method: 1) 使用几何感知的2D视觉骨干和预训练3D VAE分别编码图像2D块和点云3D令牌；2) 将两者映射到共享空间，实现局部像素-点对齐与全局语义对齐；3) 引入三阶段渐进训练策略稳定联合学习。

Result: 成功实现了对象级像素到点对齐，同时保持竞争力的全局检索性能，这是现有3D基础模型无法同时具备的能力。

Conclusion: Tango3D通过建立细粒度对齐特征空间，为纯几何3D令牌注入丰富语义，为各类密集3D下游任务铺平道路。

Abstract: Existing 3D foundation models typically align point clouds to frozen vision-language spaces like CLIP, which achieve strong cross-modal retrieval by compressing 3D shape into a global vector. However, this global-only alignment cannot establish fine-grained pixel-to-point correspondence. To solve this, we present Tango3D, a foundation model that unifies dense correspondence and global retrieval. We use a geometry-aware 2D visual backbone and a pretrained 3D VAE to encode images into 2D patches and point clouds into 3D tokens. These are mapped into a single shared space to achieve both local pixel-to-point alignment and global semantic alignment. To stabilize the joint learning of dense and global objectives, we introduce a three-stage progressive training strategy. Experiments show our model successfully achieves object-level pixel-to-point alignment while maintaining competitive global retrieval, a joint capability not offered by existing 3D foundation models. By establishing a fine-grained alignment feature space, Tango3D injects rich semantics into purely geometric 3D tokens, paving the way for a wide range of dense 3D downstream tasks.

</details>


### [54] [GeoMamba: A Geometry-driven MambaVision Framework and Dataset for Fine-grained Optical-SAR Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19734)
*Tiantong Fang,Xiuwei Wang,Jing Xiao,Wujie Zhou,Liang Liao,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMamba，一种面向光学-SAR跨模态细粒度目标检索的几何驱动框架，通过几何特征注入、一致性约束和深度监督策略，在未对齐的真实遥感场景下显著提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源遥感影像可提供互补观测信息，但光学与SAR之间因模态差异大、相干斑噪声严重、结构不一致等问题，在未对齐条件下跨模态细粒度目标检索极具挑战，现有方法难以学习鲁棒的跨模态表征。

Method: 提出GeoMamba框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 几何特征注入(GFI)模块，增强跨模态特征交互并引入结构先验；(2) 几何一致性约束(GCC)模块，结合深度监督(DS)策略，利用经典算子施加层次化几何约束；(3) 构建了包含11个航空航天与海洋类别的FGOS-as数据集用于评估。

Result: 在FGOS-as数据集上的全检索设定下，GeoMamba达到63.3% mAP和77.0% Rank-1准确率，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GeoMamba通过几何驱动的表征学习有效缓解了光学-SAR跨模态未对齐检索问题，验证了几何先验和层次化约束在跨模态遥感检索中的重要性。

Abstract: Multi-source remote sensing enables complementary observation of ground objects, while cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval remains challenging, especially under unaligned optical and SAR conditions. Unlike conventional retrieval settings that rely on paired or spatially aligned samples, practical optical-SAR retrieval is affected by substantial modality discrepancy, speckle noise, and structural inconsistency, which limit robust cross-modal representation learning. To address this problem, we propose GeoMamba, a geometry-driven framework tailored for optical-SAR fine-grained retrieval. Specifically, GeoMamba introduces a Geometric Feature Injection (GFI) module that enhances cross-modal feature interaction and incorporates structural priors, thereby improving the robustness of SAR representations and promoting geometry-consistent feature learning. In addition, a Geometric Consistency Constraint (GCC) module, together with a Deep Supervision (DS) strategy, imposes hierarchical geometric constraints using classical operators, which helps preserve informative object structures during representation learning. We further construct a new dataset, FGOS-as, containing 11 aerospace and maritime categories for evaluating unaligned cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval in realistic remote sensing scenarios. Extensive experiments on FGOS-as demonstrate that GeoMamba outperforms existing methods, achieving 63.3% mAP and 77.0% Rank-1 accuracy in all-to-all retrieval setting.

</details>


### [55] [FlowErase-RL: Rethinking Concept Erasure as Reward Optimization in Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19739)
*Yi Sun,Zhiqi Zhang,Xinhao Zhong,Yimin Zhou,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlowErase-RL，首个基于GRPO的流匹配模型概念擦除框架，通过动态双路径奖励机制优化擦除效果与生成保真度，在多个任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型在提升文本到图像生成质量的同时引入了安全风险，现有概念擦除方法存在效果有限或依赖精确标注数据、难以扩展等问题。

Method: 提出GRPO框架FlowErase-RL，将概念擦除重构为奖励优化问题，设计动态双路径奖励机制，联合优化概念擦除奖励和非目标空间奖励，并通过性能驱动的切换策略自适应平衡。

Result: 在裸露、物体和艺术风格擦除任务上达到SOTA，保持良好图像质量和语义对齐，对对抗攻击鲁棒，且能扩展到多概念场景。

Conclusion: 为流匹配模型的安全可控生成建立了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow matching models have significantly improved text-to-image generation quality, but also introduce growing safety risks due to the generation of harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods are either inference-time interventions with limited effectiveness or rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which requires precisely aligned data and struggles with scalability and multi-concept settings. In this paper, we propose \emph{FlowErase-RL}, the first GRPO-based framework for concept erasure in flow matching models. We reformulate concept erasure as a reward optimization problem and introduce a \textbf{dynamic dual-path reward mechanism} that jointly optimizes (i) a Concept Erasure (CE) reward to suppress target concepts and (ii) a Non-target Space (NS) reward to preserve generative fidelity. The two reward paths are adaptively balanced during training via a performance-driven switching strategy, enabling stable optimization without explicit supervision. Extensive experiments on nudity, object, and artistic style erasure demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art erasure performance while maintaining strong image quality and semantic alignment. Moreover, it exhibits robust resistance to adversarial attacks and scales effectively to multi-concept scenarios. Our results establish a new paradigm for safe and controllable generation in flow matching models.

</details>


### [56] [Real-World On-Vehicle Evaluation of Embedding-Based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19744)
*Albert Schotschneider,Daniel Bogdoll,Svetlana Pavlitska,Ahmed Abouelazm,Johann Marius Zoellner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练视觉Transformer嵌入的实时异常检测方法，通过单张参考图像的最近邻相似度来检测交通场景中的异常，无需显式监督或数据集特定训练，在标准基准和真实自动驾驶场景中均取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法高度特化且依赖Cityscapes等抽象语义类定义的正态性，难以适应多样化的真实世界场景；同时收集代表性异常数据具有挑战性。

Method: 利用预训练视觉Transformer（基础模型）提取的嵌入特征，通过逐块处理的最近邻相似度在潜在语义特征空间中检测偏差，仅需单张参考图像即可建模正态性，生成稠密异常掩码实现异常定位。

Result: 在Road Anomaly基准上取得了良好性能，并在真实自动驾驶车辆实验中表现出一致的定性行为，成功突出显示各种场景中的语义异常物体。

Conclusion: 简单、基于参考的方法在现实操作条件下能够提供有用的异常信号，为自动驾驶中的实时异常检测提供了一种实用且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in traffic scenes is crucial for ensuring safety in autonomous driving, yet collecting representative anomalous data remains challenging. Existing anomaly detection methods are highly specialized and rely on normality as defined by the abstract semantic Cityscapes classes, making it difficult to adapt to diverse real-world scenarios. We propose an adaptable real-time anomaly detection method that leverages foundation models in the form of pretrained vision transformer embeddings to detect deviations via nearest-neighbor similarity in the latent semantic feature space. Based on patch-wise processing, the algorithm produces dense anomaly masks, allowing for the localization of detected anomalies. The method robustly models normality through a single reference image. This formulation avoids explicit supervision and dataset-specific training, making it suitable for real-world deployment. We evaluate the method on standard benchmarks and on an automated vehicle in real-world scenarios. Despite its simplicity, the method achieves good performance on the Road Anomaly benchmark and demonstrates consistent qualitative behavior in practice, successfully highlighting semantically unusual objects in diverse scenes. These results suggest that simple, reference-based methods can provide useful anomaly signals under realistic operating conditions.

</details>


### [57] [CPC-VAR:Continual Personalized and Compositional Generation in Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19750)
*Junhao Li,Xinhao Zhong,Yi sun,Yuxia Qiao,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向VAR模型的持续个性化生成框架，通过梯度概念神经元选择（GCNS）解决顺序学习中的灾难性遗忘，并利用上下文感知合成策略实现多概念的可控融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR个性化方法局限于静态设定，无法适应持续变化的需求；顺序概念学习存在严重遗忘，多概念合成存在特征纠缠和属性不一致问题。

Method: 提出统一框架：1) GCNS识别概念相关神经元并仅约束冲突参数，缓解遗忘；2) 上下文感知合成策略，通过多分支特征建模和空间条件引导的局部交叉注意力融合，实现解耦概念合成。

Result: 在长序列持续个性化任务中显著提升性能，并在多概念图像合成上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 证明了VAR模型在可扩展、可控的个性化生成任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Visual autoregressive (VAR) models have recently emerged as an efficient paradigm for text-to-image generation. Despite their strong generative capability, existing VAR-based personalization methods remain limited to static settings, failing to accommodate evolving user demands. In particular, sequential concept learning leads to severe catastrophic forgetting, while multi-concept synthesis often suffers from feature entanglement and attribute inconsistency. In this work, we present the first systematic study of continual personalized generation in VAR models. We identify two key challenges: (i) preserving previously learned concepts during sequential customization, and (ii) composing multiple personalized concepts in a controllable manner. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework with two core components. For continual single-concept learning, we introduce Gradient-based Concept Neuron Selection (GCNS), which identifies concept-relevant neurons and constrains only conflicting parameters across tasks, effectively mitigating forgetting without additional model expansion. For multi-concept synthesis, we propose a context-aware composition strategy that performs multi-branch feature modeling and localized cross-attention fusion guided by spatial conditions, enabling precise and disentangled concept composition. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves performance in long-sequence continual personalization while achieving superior results in multi-concept image synthesis compared to existing baselines. These findings highlight the potential of VAR models for scalable and controllable personalized generation.

</details>


### [58] [Preferences Order, Ratings Anchor: From Fused Expert Aesthetic Ground Truth to Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19776)
*Yuanpei Zhao,Jie Lin,Chao Zhang,Yilin Wang,Mao Li,Chenhui Li,Jie Hou,Tangjie Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PPaint数据集，采用匹配的成对偏好和点式评分双协议对150幅中国画进行美学评估，发现两种协议具有互补优势。基于偏好转分数的方法，进一步提出了PSDistill训练框架，使Qwen3-VL-8B在单类别训练后SRCC从0.504提升至0.709，超越所有开源基线，接近闭源Gemini-3.1-Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像美学评估基准仅采用单一标注协议（成对偏好或点式评分），缺乏在受控条件下对两者互补性的定量测量。

Method: 1) 构建PPaint数据集：15位领域专家对150幅中国画在五个美学维度上同时进行成对偏好和点式评分标注，收集45,900个偏好判断。2) 提出PSDistill方法：将VLM的成对判断通过Elo参考池转换为校准的伪分数，再利用置信度加权排序优化训练同一VLM，使其成为单次前向推理的美学评分器。

Result: 在单类别中国画上训练后，Qwen3-VL-8B的SRCC从0.504提升至0.709，超越所有开源基线（包括专用美学模型ArtiMuse），并以单次推理成本达到与闭源Gemini-3.1-Pro仅差0.04 SRCC的性能，跨域迁移在APDDv2上得到验证。

Conclusion: 成对偏好和点式评分具有互补优势，偏好提供更一致的序数排名，评分锚定绝对分数尺度。融合两者的偏好转分数方法可生成收敛一致的专家真值，且该原理可扩展至免标签的VLM训练，显著提升美学评分性能。

Abstract: Pairwise preferences and pointwise ratings are the two dominant annotation protocols in image aesthetic assessment (IAA), yet existing benchmarks adopt only one, leaving their complementarity unmeasured under controlled conditions. We introduce PPaint, a matched dual-protocol benchmark in which 15 domain experts, 5 per category, annotate 150 Chinese paintings under both protocols across five aesthetic dimensions, collecting 45,900 pairwise expert judgments through a locally dense preference design alongside the matched ratings. The matched design reveals complementary strengths: preferences yield more consistent ordinal rankings, while ratings anchor the absolute score scale. Fusing both signals via two independent preference-to-score methods yields a fused expert ground truth on which the two constructions converge to nearly identical scores. The same preference-to-score principle extends to label-free VLM training. PSDistill converts VLM pairwise judgments into calibrated pseudo-scores via an Elo reference pool, and trains the same VLM with confidence-weighted ranking optimization to produce a single-pass aesthetic scorer. Trained on a single painting category, the distilled Qwen3-VL-8B improves mean SRCC from 0.504 to 0.709 across all three categories, outperforming all open-source baselines including the dedicated aesthetic model ArtiMuse and matching closed-source Gemini-3.1-Pro within 0.04 SRCC at single-pass inference cost, with cross-domain transfer further validated on APDDv2. We will release the full PPaint dataset and training code.

</details>


### [59] [Fast 4D Mesh Generation by Spatio-Temporal Attention Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19786)
*Dvir Samuel,Yuval Atzmon,Gal Chechik,Yoni Kasten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的4D网格生成加速方法，利用时空注意力链在9秒内生成4D网格，实现13倍加速，并支持更长视频序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D网格生成方法速度慢、计算成本高，难以扩展到长视频序列。

Method: 提出时空注意力链框架，通过锚点网格顶点映射到潜在token，在潜在空间追踪时序对应关系，再通过潜在到顶点的注意力恢复每帧顶点。

Result: 9秒生成4D网格，实现13倍加速；支持16倍更长视频序列且不降低网格质量；在2D目标跟踪和4D跟踪任务上取得零样本竞争性能。

Conclusion: 方法高效加速4D网格生成，保持高质量的同时支持长序列和下游任务应用（跟踪、相机估计）。

Abstract: 4D mesh generation has recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for recovering dynamic 3D structure from videos, but existing methods remain slow, computationally expensive, and difficult to scale to longer sequences. We introduce a training-free approach that accelerates 4D mesh generation while improving temporal correspondence quality. Our key observation is that temporal correspondences emerge inside a 4D backbone long before its generated meshes become visually accurate. We exploit this with a general framework we call Spatio-Temporal Attention Chain which propagates information across space and time. Starting from vertices on an anchor mesh, the chain maps vertices to latent tokens. It then follows temporal correspondences in latent space, and recovers frame-specific vertices through latent-to-vertex attention. This design avoids expensive explicit matching while preserving anchor mesh details and thereby improving dynamic mesh geometry and temporal consistency.
  Compared to state-of-the-art, our method generates a 4D mesh in 9 seconds, achieving a $13\times$ speedup while producing higher-quality results. Moreover, our approach scales to videos up to $16\times$ longer without degrading mesh quality. Beyond generation, the improved correspondences enable competitive zero-shot performance on two downstream tasks: 2D object tracking and 4D tracking. We further show that our framework enables reliable camera estimation, a capability not supported by prior 4D mesh generation methods.

</details>


### [60] [Mechanisms of Object Localization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19792)
*Timothy Schaumlöffel,Martina G. Vilas,Gemma Roig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过机械可解释性工具分析了LLaVA-1.5和InternVL-3.5两类视觉语言模型，发现目标定位依赖于一种"容器化机制"：目标对齐的token定义了空间范围，而内部语义排列对预测框几乎无影响。只有极少数注意力头介导分类和定位的因果效应，两个任务共享部分早期处理但依赖不同的专用头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在分类和定位任务上表现不佳，其中定位机制的理解尤为薄弱，现有研究对此缺乏深入分析。

Method: 使用token消融、注意力头剔除和因果中介分析等机械可解释性工具，对LLaVA-1.5和InternVL-3.5两类模型进行系统分析。

Result: 定位由容器化机制驱动，目标对齐token定义空间范围，内部语义排列无关紧要；仅极少数注意力头介导因果效应，分类和定位共享部分早期处理但依赖不同的专用头。

Conclusion: 首次从层和头级别揭示了VLM中定位的计算机制，发现狭窄的计算路径，可为未来模型设计和接地目标提供指导。

Abstract: Visually-grounded language models (VLMs) are highly effective in linking visual and textual information, yet they often struggle with basic classification and localization tasks. While classification mechanisms have been studied more extensively, the processes that support object localization remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate two representative families, LLaVA-1.5 and InternVL-3.5, using a suite of mechanistic interpretability tools, including token ablations, attention knockout, and causal mediation analysis. We find that localization is driven by a containerization mechanism in which object-aligned tokens define the spatial extent of the object, while the semantic arrangement of tokens within those boundaries is largely irrelevant to the predicted box. Only a very small set of attention heads mediates the causal effect for both classification and localization, concentrating in early-mid layers for LLaVA and mid-late layers for InternVL. The two tasks share some early processing but ultimately depend on largely distinct specialized heads. Overall, we provide the first layer- and head-level account of localization in VLMs, revealing narrow computational pathways that can guide future model design and grounding objectives.

</details>


### [61] [Stitched Value Model for Diffusion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19804)
*Hyojun Go,Hyungjin Chung,Prune Truong,Goutam Bhat,Li Mi,Zhaochong An,Zixiang Zhao,Dominik Narnhofer,Serge Belongie,Federico Tombari,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StitchVM框架，通过将预训练的像素空间奖励模型与扩散模型骨干拼接，高效地将奖励函数迁移到噪声潜空间，实现轻量级扩散模型对齐，显著提升下游任务的训练速度和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型对齐面临的核心挑战是：奖励函数定义在干净图像上，但对齐过程需要噪声中间潜变量的价值函数估计。现有Tweedie估计有偏、Monte Carlo估计计算昂贵，而直接学习噪声潜变量的价值函数又效果不佳。

Method: StitchVM采用模型拼接框架：将截断的像素空间奖励模型（如CLIP ViT-L）与冻结的扩散模型骨干（如SD 3.5 Medium）拼接，形成混合模型。拼接和微调仅需10 GPU小时。

Result: DPS加速3.2倍且GPU内存减半，DiffusionNFT加速2.3倍，在多种下游对齐任务中均取得性能提升。

Conclusion: StitchVM开创了扩散模型对齐的新范式：不再对每个样本进行昂贵的逐样本价值函数近似，而是一次性构建正确的噪声潜变量价值函数，并在大量样本和迭代中摊销，实现了高效且有效的对齐。

Abstract: For practical use, diffusion- or flow-based generative models must be aligned with task-specific rewards, such as prompt fidelity or aesthetic preference. That alignment is challenging because the reward is defined for clean output images, but the alignment procedure requires value function estimates at noisy intermediate latents. Existing methods resort to Tweedie-style or Monte Carlo approximations, trading off estimator bias against computational cost: Tweedie estimates are efficient but biased, while Monte Carlo estimates are more accurate but require expensive rollouts. A natural alternative would be a learned value function, but it remains an open question how to effectively train a strong and general value model specifically for noisy latents. Here, we propose StitchVM, a model stitching framework that efficiently transfers reward models pretrained for clean images to the noisy latent regime. StitchVM starts from an existing, truncated pixel-space reward model and attaches a frozen diffusion backbone to it as its head. From the pixel-space model, the resulting hybrid retains a carefully pretrained, robust reward capability; from the diffusion backbone, it inherits its native ability to handle noisy latents. The stitching procedure is exceptionally lightweight, e.g., stitching and finetuning CLIP ViT-L and SD 3.5 Medium takes only 10 GPU-hours. By lifting powerful pixel-space reward models to latent space, StitchVM opens up a new style of diffusion alignment: instead of rough, yet costly per-sample approximation of the value function, the correct function for the actual, noisy latents is constructed once and then amortized over many samples and iterations. We show that this approach yields improvements across a broad range of downstream steering and post-training methods: DPS becomes $3.2\times$ faster while halving peak GPU memory, and DiffusionNFT becomes $2.3\times$ faster.

</details>


### [62] [When Preference Labels Fall Short: Aligning Diffusion Models from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19839)
*Weiyan Chen,Weijian Deng,Yao Xiao,Weijie Tu,ZiYi Dong,Ibrahim Radwan,Liang Lin,Pengxu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨是否可以用真实数据替代模型生成数据作为偏好对齐的监督信号，提出RealAlign方法，通过对比真实图像与生成/扰动图像构建偏好信号，无需人工标注，实验证明该方法性能与现有基于偏好的方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好对齐方法依赖模型生成图像构建的偏好对，这种监督具有相对性和模糊性，当两个样本都存在伪影或质量有限时，难以推断真正理想的输出。本文探究真实数据能否作为替代监督源。

Method: 采用数据为中心视角，设计一种数据策展策略：将真实图像作为参考点，通过对比真实图像与生成的或扰动的样本构建偏好信号，无需手动标注的偏好对。

Result: 基于真实数据的监督能为扩散模型对齐提供有效指导，性能与现有基于偏好的方法相当。

Conclusion: 真实数据为偏好对齐提供了实用且互补的监督来源，并指出了标签高效对齐策略的发展方向。

Abstract: Preference alignment aims to guide generative models by learning from comparisons between preferred and non-preferred samples. In practice, most existing approaches rely on preference pairs constructed from model-generated images. Such supervision is inherently relative and can be ambiguous when both samples exhibit artifacts or limited visual quality, making it difficult to infer what constitutes a truly desirable output. In this work, we investigate whether real data can serve as an alternative source of supervision for preference alignment. We adopt a data-centric perspective and study a curation strategy that treats real images as reference points and constructs preference signals by contrasting them with generated or perturbed samples, without requiring manually annotated preference pairs. Through empirical analysis, we show that real-data-based supervision provides effective guidance for aligning diffusion models and achieves performance comparable to existing preference-based methods. Our results suggest that real data offers a practical and complementary source of supervision for preference alignment and highlight directions of label-efficient alignment strategies. Code and models are available at https://cwyxx.github.io/RealAlign.

</details>


### [63] [A Framework for Evaluating Zero-Shot Image Generation in Concept-based Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19855)
*Giacomo Astolfi,Matteo Bianchi,Riccardo Campi,Antonio De Santis,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用零样本文本到图像生成模型合成概念数据集，用于概念可解释AI，并通过四个互补分析评估了合成概念相对于真实概念的忠实度，发现尽管有潜力但仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念可解释AI需要大量标注图像来代表每个概念，这限制了可扩展性，因此需要探索更高效的概念数据获取方式。

Method: 使用零样本T2I生成模型根据预设提示生成概念，并通过四个分析评估其忠实度：概念表示相似性、组内相似性、下游解释任务性能以及概念移除影响。

Result: 当前T2I生成模型虽可作为概念可解释AI的捷径，但研究揭示了合成数据在模型分析中的挑战和未解决的问题。

Conclusion: 零样本T2I生成模型在概念可解释AI中有应用前景，但合成概念与真实概念之间的差距仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Concept-based Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) interprets deep learning models using human-understandable visual features (e.g., textures or object parts) by linking internal representations to class predictions, thereby bridging the gap between low-level image data and high-level semantics. A major challenge, however, is the reliance on large sets of labeled images to represent each concept, which limits scalability. In this work, we investigate the use of zero-shot Text-to-Image (T2I) generative models as a source of synthetic concept datasets for concept-based XAI methods. Specifically, we generate concepts using predefined prompts and evaluate their faithfulness to real ones through four complementary analyses: (1) comparing synthetic vs. real concept images via concept representation similarity; (2) evaluating their intra-similarity by comparing pairs of subsets of the same concept with progressively increasing size; (3) evaluating their performance for downstream explanation tasks using relevant class images; (4) evaluating how removing a concept from tested class images affects explanations of generated concepts. While current T2I generative models promise a shortcut to concept-based XAI, our study highlights challenges and raises open questions about the use of synthetic data generated by zero-shot pipelines in model analyses. The resulting dataset is available at https://github.com/DataSciencePolimi/ZeroShot-T2I-Concepts.

</details>


### [64] [Eyes on VLM: Benchmarking Gaze Following and Social Gaze Prediction in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19859)
*Hengfei Wang,Anshul Gupta,Pierre Vuillecard,Jean-Marc Odobez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EyeVLM，一个系统评估视觉语言模型（VLM）在人类注视理解能力上的框架，涵盖注视跟随和社交注视预测两个核心任务，并通过零样本测试和微调两种方式评估。结果表明当前VLM在注视理解方面能力不足，即使经过训练仍与纯视觉模型存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs已成为强大的多模态推理器，但在人类注视与注意力分析这一理解人类行为的关键任务上的能力尚未被系统评估。现有研究缺乏对VLM在注视理解方面的全面评测。

Method: 提出EyeVLM评估框架，包含两个任务：注视跟随（几何与视觉处理）和社交注视预测（社交与关系推理）。评估方式包括零样本设置（多种提示策略）和基于任务特定QA对的微调，并在现有注视数据集上与纯视觉模型进行系统比较。

Result: 当前VLM在注视理解任务上表现不足，缺乏精确的注视理解能力。标准训练虽能缩小与纯视觉模型的差距，但仍有显著改进空间。

Conclusion: VLM在人类注视理解这一精细视觉任务上尚不具备可靠能力，需要进一步的研究和提升。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have rapidly evolved into general-purpose multimodal reasoners with strong zero-shot generalization. In this context, VLMs could greatly benefit the analysis of human gaze and attention, a central task in human behavior understanding that requires reasoning about the physical scene as well as the activity, interactions, and social context. However, the extent to which VLMs can reliably understand human gaze and related attentional behaviors remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present EyeVLM, a systematic evaluation framework for gaze understanding in VLMs across two complementary dimensions: tasks and models. To assess gaze understanding capabilities, we focus on two core tasks. The first, gaze following, i.e., predicting the 2D location where a person is looking, has a geometric and visual processing focus, requiring a precise understanding of the human face, attention direction, 3D scene structure, and spatial grounding of attended targets. The second, social gaze prediction, requires social and relational reasoning over multi-person interactions (e.g., mutual gaze and shared attention), and may benefit more from the LLM semantic reasoning capabilities within VLMs. Regarding models, EyeVLM evaluates these tasks in two ways: a zero-shot setting with a diverse set of state-of-the-art open- and closed-source VLMs, exploring different prompting strategies; and a fine-tuning approach based on task-specific QA pairs, studying the impact of model scale and data scale. As benchmarks, we rely on existing gaze understanding datasets and perform a systematic comparison with state-of-the-art purely visual models. Overall, our results show that current VLMs lack precise gaze understanding capabilities. While standard training helps reduce the gap with visual models, significant improvements are still needed.

</details>


### [65] [Landscape-Awareness for Geometric View Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19865)
*Yan-Ting Chen,Hao-Wei Chen,Tsu-Ching Hsiao,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分数的优化方法，结合视点条件扩散模型，在稀疏视图条件下实现更准确、更高效的双视图相机位姿估计，解决了现有方法因损失函数非凸导致对初始化敏感和需大量采样的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型（如Zero123）的视点估计方法在稀疏视图（尤其是双视图）条件下，因损失函数非凸、存在大量局部极小值，导致优化对初始化敏感，且依赖朴素的多起点策略，几何歧义（对称性、自相似性）会误导梯度更新至错误视点。

Method: 提出一种分数引导的优化方法：首先利用基于分数的方法重塑优化景观，引导梯度更新朝向真实视点；然后使用视点条件扩散模型进行精细化调整。

Result: 实验表明，该方法改善了收敛性，减少了对暴力采样的依赖，在更高样本效率下达到了具有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的分数引导+精细化两阶段方法有效克服了稀疏视图下视点估计的非凸优化难题，兼顾了精度与效率。

Abstract: Accurate camera viewpoint estimation under sparse-view conditions remains challenging, particularly in two-view scenarios. Recent approaches leverage diffusion models such as Zero123 to synthesize novel views conditioned on relative viewpoint, showing promising results when repurposed for viewpoint estimation via optimization with MSE loss. However, existing methods often suffer from nonconvex loss landscape with numerous local minima, making them sensitive to initialization and reliant on naive multistart strategies. We analyze these optimization challenges and visualize failure cases, showing that geometric ambiguities, such as symmetry and self-similarity, can mislead gradient-based updates toward incorrect viewpoints. To address these limitations, we propose a score-based method that reshapes the optimization landscape to guide updates toward the ground-truth viewpoint, followed by a refinement stage using a viewpoint-conditioned diffusion model. Experiments show that our method improves convergence, reduces reliance on brute-force sampling, and achieves competitive accuracy with higher sample-efficiency.

</details>


### [66] [Structured Layout Priors for Robust Out-of-Distribution Visual Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19866)
*Peter El Hachem,Ahmed Nassar,A. Said Gurbuz,Christoph Auer,Peter W. J. Staar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过轻量级检测器预解析布局实体，以DocTags词汇注入VLM解码器，解决文档解析中因布局分布外导致的两跳瓶颈问题，显著提升性能且仅增加少量延迟和token开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM在端到端解析文档时，对于训练中未见过的布局容易失效，原因是存在两跳瓶颈：解码器必须先分类定位布局实体（第一跳），然后才能提取内容（第二跳），第一跳失败会导致第二跳崩溃。作者希望在不改变VLM架构的前提下解决这一问题。

Method: 在解码器外部使用轻量级RT-DETR检测器预解析布局实体，将其输出序列化为模型的DocTags词汇，并与完整页面图像一起注入prompt。检测结果作为解码器的先验知识共享其生成空间，同时保留全图作为检测噪声时的回退。

Result: 在10k页结构OOD基准上，Markdown F1从0.37提升至0.92；在OmniDocBench中文子集上，表格TEDS从0.01提升至0.36；在26k页ViDoRe V3基准上，无限循环解码故障在所有工业领域均下降。仅增加15%延迟和74个token的prompt开销。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了两跳瓶颈，注意力分析进一步证实了解码器在生成结构时关注注入的布局token、生成内容时关注图像块的双峰相位转移，表明布局先验与视觉信息形成了有效互补。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) parse documents end-to-end but frequently break down on layouts unlike those seen in training. We attribute this to a two-hop bottleneck: before the decoder can extract content (Hop 2), it must first classify and localize the enclosing layout entity (Hop 1), and when the first hop fails the second collapses into omissions, malformed structure, or autoregressive repetition. We pre-resolve Hop 1 outside the decoder by running a lightweight RT-DETR detector, serializing its outputs in the parser's native DocTags vocabulary, and injecting them into the prompt alongside the full page image. Unlike analyze-then-parse approaches that crop the page, or prior prompt-level priors written in plain text, our prior shares the decoder's generation space and leaves the global image in view as a fallback when detections are noisy. On a 10k-page structural out-of-distribution benchmark, markdown F1 rises from $0.37$ to $0.92$; on the Chinese subset of OmniDocBench, table TEDS rises from $0.01$ to $0.36$; and on the 26k-page ViDoRe V3 benchmark, infinite-loop decoding failures drop across every industrial domain tested. These gains cost $15\%$ wall-clock latency and a median of $74$ prompt tokens, with no architectural change to the base VLM. An attention-level analysis further reveals a bimodal phase shift in which the decoder attends to injected layout tokens when emitting structure and to image patches when emitting content, consistent with the two-hop bottleneck being alleviated. Model weights will be released to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [67] [WoundFormer: Multi-Scale Spatial Feature Fusion for Multi-Class Wound Tissue Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19868)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Rabin Dulal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WoundFormer，一种基于Transformer的多类伤口组织分割框架，通过空间保持的多尺度聚合头提升分层特征融合，在两类数据集上达到81.9% Dice，超越基线最高4.3个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性伤口（如糖尿病足溃疡、压力性损伤）需要精确的组织层面评估以指导治疗，但现有深度学习方法多聚焦于二值分割，因组织间高度类内变异和标注数据有限，难以有效建模异质性组织构成。

Method: 基于Transformer的WoundFormer框架，用空间保持的多尺度聚合头替换标准SegFormer解码器，在跨尺度集成时维持特征拓扑，并通过卷积融合增强上下文交互，改进边界定位和相似组织类别的判别能力。

Result: 在WoundTissueSeg数据集（147张图像，6类组织）上总体Dice达81.9%，超越CNN和Transformer基线最高4.3个Dice点，在少数类组织上也有持续提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模分层空间交互能增强Transformer在异质性伤口组织分割中的表示能力，支持更可靠的定量伤口评估。

Abstract: Chronic wounds such as diabetic foot ulcers and pressure injuries require accurate tissue-level assessment to guide treatment planning and monitor healing progression. While deep learning methods have advanced automated wound analysis, most existing approaches focus on binary segmentation and inadequately model heterogeneous tissue composition due to high intra-class variability and limited annotated data. Multi-class wound tissue segmentation, therefore, remains a challenging and clinically relevant problem. We propose WoundFormer, a transformer-based framework that enhances hierarchical spatial feature fusion for multi-class wound tissue segmentation. Specifically, we replace the standard SegFormer decoder with a spatially-preserving multi-scale aggregation head that maintains feature topology during cross-scale integration and strengthens contextual interactions through convolutional fusion. This design improves boundary localization and discrimination between visually similar tissue categories while preserving transformer efficiency. We evaluate WoundFormer on the WoundTissueSeg dataset (147 images, six tissue classes) and a second benchmark (DFUTissue dataset). The proposed method achieves an overall Dice score of 81.9%, outperforming strong CNN- and transformer-based baselines by up to 4.3 Dice points on the WoundTissueSeg benchmark, with consistent improvements across minority tissue classes. These results indicate that explicit modeling of hierarchical spatial interactions enhances transformer representations for heterogeneous wound tissue segmentation and supports more reliable quantitative wound assessment.

</details>


### [68] [Passive Construction Site Safety Monitoring via Persona-Scaffolded Adversarial Chain-of-Thought VLM Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19869)
*Ananth Sriram,Neel Mokaria,Rajveer Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多阶段视频分析的被动式施工安全监控系统，利用YOLO11、SAM 3和Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型，通过三阶段架构实现PPE检测、违规识别与OSHA标准映射，其中第三阶段的对抗式链式思维提示设计将精度提升12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国建筑业安全事故频发（2023年1055例死亡，多数可预防），现有监控方法成本高、需实时人工干预或仅覆盖少数违规类型。

Method: 三阶段管道：①微调YOLO11检测PPE和危险物；②SAM 3分割优化与工人去重；③Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct结合方法提示、角色框架和三遍对抗式链式思维协议进行合规验证与幻觉控制。

Result: 在12段视频的Ironsite开发集上，Stage 3的对抗式提示设计相比单次提示将精度提升12%，尤其在易产生幻觉的违规类别上改善最显著。

Conclusion: 系统可自动将违规映射至OSHA标准、进行REBA人体工学风险评分，并生成带时间戳证据的工人安全报告；已发布评估工具供后续复现。

Abstract: Construction remains the deadliest industry sector in the United States, with 1,055 fatal worker injuries recorded in 2023, and the majority preventable. Existing monitoring approaches are expensive, require real-time human operators, or address only a narrow subset of violations. This paper presents a passive, end-of-shift construction safety monitoring pipeline processing video from POV body-worn and fixed wall-mounted cameras through a three-stage architecture: (1) fine-tuned YOLO11 for primary PPE and hazard detection, (2) SAM 3 for segmentation refinement and worker deduplication, and (3) Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct with a method-prompted, persona-scaffolded three-pass adversarial chain-of-thought protocol for compliance verification and hallucination control. The principal contribution is the Stage 3 prompt design: professional persona backstories following the method-actor framing drive an observed 12% precision improvement over single-pass prompting in an informal three-author review of the 12-video Ironsite development corpus, with the largest gains on hallucination-prone violation categories. Structural message isolation enforces observational independence between a generator, discriminator, and reconciliation pass governed by asymmetric rules encoding priors about human observation versus automated detection reliability. The system maps violations to OSHA standards, performs REBA-inspired ergonomic risk scoring from pose keypoints, and produces per-worker safety reports with timestamped evidence. An evaluation harness is released for future reproduction.

</details>


### [69] [Structural Energy Guidance for View-Consistent Text-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19876)
*Qing Zhang,Jinguang Tong,Jing Zhang,Jie Hong,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEGS框架，通过在U-Net特征的PCA子空间中构建结构能量并注入梯度到去噪过程，无需训练即可缓解文本到3D生成中的Janus问题，将Janus率降低约10%并提升多视角一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到3D生成中基于扩散模型的方法常出现Janus问题（多视角几何不一致），根本原因是2D扩散先验存在视角偏差，需要一种无需重新训练的高效解决方案。

Method: 在U-Net特征的PCA子空间中构建结构能量，将其梯度注入到去噪过程中，可无缝集成到SDS/VSD流水线中，无需重新训练。

Result: 在DreamFusion、Magic3D、LucidDreamer等多个基线上，Janus率平均降低约10%，View-CS分数提升，同时保持外观保真度。

Conclusion: SEGS有效缓解视角伪影，为高质量文本到3D内容生成提供了一种灵活、即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-3D generation based on diffusion models often suffers from the Janus problem, leading to inconsistent geometry across viewpoints. This work identifies viewpoint bias in 2D diffusion priors as the main cause and proposes Structural Energy-Guided Sampling (SEGS), a training-free and plug-and-play framework to improve multi-view consistency. SEGS constructs a structural energy in the PCA subspace of U-Net features and injects its gradient into the denoising process. It can be easily integrated into SDS/VSD pipelines without retraining. Experiments show that SEGS reduces the Janus Rate by about 10% on average and improves View-CS scores across multiple baselines, including DreamFusion, Magic3D, and LucidDreamer. This method effectively alleviates viewpoint artifacts while preserving appearance fidelity, providing a flexible solution for high-quality text-to-3D content generation.

</details>


### [70] [GoTTA be Diverse: Rethinking Memory Policies for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19890)
*Shyma Alhuwaider,Yasmeen Alsaedy,Merey Ramazanova,Silvio Giancola,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地将测试时自适应（TTA）中的内存策略与自适应算法解耦，提出了一种关注类内多样性的内存管理方法GOTTA，实验表明多样性感知的内存策略在非独立同分布和约束内存条件下显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA研究中内存机制通常作为特定算法的一部分被评估，难以孤立分析内存设计选择的影响，缺乏统一、系统性的内存策略基准。

Method: 提出GOTTA（Guided Observational Test-Time Adaptation）系列内存策略，结合类平衡分配与特征空间多样性，可作为即插即用模块替换现有缓冲区，并在i.i.d.、非i.i.d.、持续学习和实际测试流等统一条件下进行评估。

Result: 在损坏基准和视频流场景下，多样性感知的内存在内存预算受限和具有挑战性的非i.i.d.流中改进最为显著，随着内存容量增加仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 内存管理应被视为鲁棒测试时自适应的第一级组件，多样性是实现实际TTA的核心原则。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) enables a pre-trained model to adapt online to an unlabeled test stream under distribution shift. While most TTA research focuses on the adaptation objective, practical streams also depend critically on the memory used to select which test samples drive adaptation. Existing memory mechanisms are usually evaluated as components of specific TTA algorithms, making it difficult to isolate which memory design choices matter and when they matter. In this work, we provide a systematic benchmark that decouples memory from the adaptation algorithm and evaluates memory policies under unified conditions across i.i.d., non-i.i.d., continual, and practical test streams. Our study shows that effective memory management requires more than retaining recent or class-balanced samples. In particular, intra-class diversity is a key factor for avoiding redundant buffers and maintaining representative adaptation signals under temporally correlated and label-skewed streams. Motivated by this finding, we introduce Guided Observational Test-Time Adaptation (GOTTA), a family of diversity-aware memory policies that combine class-balanced allocation with feature-space diversity. GOTTA memories act as drop-in replacements for existing buffers and can be paired with different TTA objectives. Across corruption benchmarks and video-stream settings, diversity-aware memory improves adaptation most clearly under constrained memory budgets and challenging non-i.i.d. streams, while remaining competitive as memory capacity increases. These results highlight memory management as a first-class component of robust test-time adaptation and identify diversity as a central principle for practical TTA.

</details>


### [71] [Breaking Modality Heterogeneity in Low-Bit Quantization for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19929)
*Yi Zhong,Haotong Qin,Xindong Zhang,Lei Zhang,Guolei Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplitQ是一种基于通道分离的后训练量化框架，通过分离模态特异性异常通道和跨模态校准，在低比特量化下显著提升视觉语言模型的性能，在W3A3设置下保留93.5%的FP16精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法因文本和视觉模态的激活分布异构导致精度下降，这种跨模态异构在通道间分布不均：少量通道包含大部分模态特异性异常值，且不同模态的异常值位于不同通道。

Method: 提出SplitQ框架，包含两个核心模块：1）模态特异性异常通道解耦（MOCD）模块，有效隔离显著的异常通道；2）自适应跨模态校准（ACC）模块，采用轻量级可学习双分支动态缓解模态引起的量化误差。

Result: 在6个多模态数据集上，SplitQ在W4A8、W4A4、W3A3、W3A2等所有量化设置下均显著优于现有方法。在W3A3极端设置下达到69.5（FP16为74.3），保留93.5%的性能。

Conclusion: SplitQ有效解决了VLM量化中的跨模态异构问题，为在资源受限设备上部署先进VLM提供了高效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Low-bit post-training quantization (PTQ) is a pivotal technique for deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on resource-constrained devices. However, existing PTQ methods often degrade VLMs' accuracy due to the heterogeneous activation distributions of text and vision modalities during quantization. We find that this cross-modal heterogeneity is distributed unevenly across channels: a small subset of channels contains most modality-specific outliers, and these outliers typically reside in different channels for each modality. Motivated by this, we propose SplitQ, a channel-Splitting-driven post-training Quantization framework. At its core, SplitQ introduces a novel Modality-specific Outlier Channel Decoupling (MOCD) module that effectively isolates salient modality-specific outlier channels with minimal overhead. To further address the remaining cross-modal distribution discrepancies, we design an Adaptive Cross-Modal Calibration (ACC) module that employs dual lightweight learnable branches to dynamically mitigate modality-induced quantization errors. Extensive experiments on popular VLMs demonstrate that SplitQ significantly outperforms existing approaches across 6 popular multi-modal datasets under all evaluated quantization settings, including W4A8, W4A4, W3A3, and W3A2. Notably, SplitQ preserves 93.5% of FP16 performance under the challenging W3A3 setting (69.5 vs. 74.3), pushing the efficiency frontier for deploying advanced VLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/EMVision-NK/SplitQ

</details>


### [72] [StruMPL: Multi-task Dense Regression under Disjoint Partial Supervision and MNAR Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19931)
*Reza M. Asiyabi,Juan Alberto Molina-Valero,The SEOSAW Partnership,Steven Hancock,Casey M. Ryan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StruMPL框架，通过多任务密集回归、异质部分监督、MNAR标签校正和物理约束，联合利用星载激光雷达和地面样地数据估算森林地上生物量(AGB)，在两种生态区上优于现有方法，高AGB偏差降低约54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载激光雷达提供结构数据但无生物量，地面样地提供生物量但有偏且无结构，两种数据源标签不兼容、标签非随机缺失(MNAR)，且生物量与结构变量间存在已知异速生长关系，需要统一框架联合利用这些异构数据源。

Method: 提出StruMPL框架：(1)共享编码器连接多个回归头、插补头和倾向性头进行空间MNAR校正；(2)可学习的物理模块在每像素评估任务间物理约束；(3)监督损失使用Augmented IPW (AIPW)伪结果，并对倾向性和插补基线使用停止梯度，确保联合优化恢复IPW加权驻点并保持有界损失。

Result: 在两个生态不同的生物群落上，StruMPL在AGB RMSE和偏差上优于消融变体和最接近的现有方法，分层分析显示AIPW将高AGB偏差降低约54%。

Conclusion: StruMPL有效解决了异构部分监督下多任务密集回归问题，通过物理约束和MNAR校正显著提升了森林AGB估算的准确性和无偏性。

Abstract: Estimating forest aboveground biomass (AGB) from Earth observation combines two structurally incompatible label sources: spaceborne lidar provides canopy structure at millions of locations but no biomass estimate, and ground-based plots provide biomass at thousands of biased locations but no metrics of structure. No single training sample carries labels for all target variables, plot labels are missing not at random (MNAR), and biomass is linked to the structural variables by known but biome-specific allometric laws. We formalise this as multi-task dense regression under heterogeneous disjoint partial supervision with MNAR labels and inter-task physical constraints, and propose StruMPL to address it jointly. A shared encoder feeds per-variable regression, imputation, and propensity heads for spatial MNAR correction, and a learnable physics module that evaluates the inter-task constraint on the model's own predictions at every pixel. The supervised loss uses an Augmented IPW (AIPW) pseudo-outcome with stop-gradients on the propensity and on the imputation baseline; we show analytically and empirically that both are necessary for joint optimisation to recover IPW-weighted stationary points while keeping the loss bounded. On two ecologically distinct biomes, StruMPL outperforms ablation variants and the closest published method on AGB RMSE and bias, with a stratified analysis showing AIPW reduces high-AGB bias by ~54%.

</details>


### [73] [Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting from Sparse Aerial Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19949)
*Dongli Wu,Zhuoxiao Li,Tongyan Hua,Yinrui Ren,Xiaobao Wei,Rongjun Qin,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyCity是一个面向稀疏航拍城市场景的观测引导生成式重建框架，通过预测观测支持的几何潜在表示、使用支架条件的航拍补全令牌进行门控残差更新，并结合密集到稀疏蒸馏与航拍自适应视频扩散先验，实现从稀疏视角一次性前馈高质量3D高斯重建，在合成与真实场景上均优于前馈基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏航拍视角重建大规模城市场景时，由于自上而下和浅斜视角导致的观测证据不平衡（屋顶与开阔区域重复观测，而立面、远处建筑和遮挡结构缺乏多视图支持），现有前馈3D高斯方法直接回归确定性表示会导致鬼影、立面融化与纹理拉伸等问题。

Method: 1) 预测观测支持的几何潜在表示以锚定可靠结构；2) 使用支架条件航拍补全令牌预测弱约束内容的门控残差更新；3) 训练时采用密集到稀疏蒸馏迁移结构线索，以及航拍自适应视频扩散先验提供精细外观线索；4) 观测保持目标函数确保精化表示与输入支持的几何一致。

Result: 在合成场景、航拍域、无人机纹理场景以及真实世界场景上，AnyCity一致性地优于前馈基线方法，实现连贯的城市新视角合成且推理时间仅需秒级。

Conclusion: AnyCity通过明确的观测几何与先验驱动内容分离策略，结合多种训练信号约束，有效解决了稀疏航拍重建中的证据不平衡问题，实现了高质量、高效的城市级场景3D重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing large-scale urban scenes from sparse aerial views is a crucial yet challenging task. Due to biased top-down and shallow-oblique camera poses, sparse aerial captures exhibit strong evidence imbalance: roofs and open regions are repeatedly observed, while facades, distant buildings, and occluded structures receive little multi-view support. Existing feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting methods directly regress a deterministic representation from sparse inputs, but this often leads to ghosting, melted facades, and stretched textures. Recent pseudo-view and video-based generative reconstruction methods use additional supervision or generative priors. However, they often lack a clear separation between observed geometry and prior-driven content, which can lead to plausible but inconsistent structures. We propose AnyCity, an observation-grounded generative reconstruction framework for sparse aerial urban scenes. AnyCity first predicts an observation-supported geometry latent to anchor reliable structures, and then uses scaffold-conditioned aerial completion tokens to predict a gated residual update for weakly constrained content before Gaussian decoding. During training, dense-to-sparse distillation transfers structural cues from dense-view reconstruction, while an aerial-adapted video diffusion prior provides fine-grained urban appearance cues through gated token conditioning. Observation-preserving objectives keep the refined representation consistent with input-supported geometry. At inference time, AnyCity reconstructs the final 3D Gaussian scene from sparse aerial views in a single feed-forward pass, achieving coherent urban novel-view synthesis with second-level inference. Experiments on synthetic, aerial-domain, UAV-textured, and real-world scenes show consistent improvements over feed-forward baselines.

</details>
